Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the bayesian_statistics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 600 and the convergence rate is O(739/n^4).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 600 + O(1/n^739). Summing over all modes and applying the bayesian_statistics convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
